BUSINESS REVIEW AND OUTLOOK                                                業務回顧及展望

In 2025, the paper manufacturing industry in the PRC recorded a            2025 年 ， 中 國 造 紙 行 業 在 產 量 微 增 的 同
slight increase in production output amid margin pressure, showing a       時 利 潤 承 壓 ， 呈 現 出「 總 量 增 長 、 盈 利 分
pattern of “increase in total production output yet divergence in          化」的格局。受制於原材料價格波動及下
earning performance”. Affected by price fluctuations in raw materials      游需求復甦不均衡，行業整體獲利能力下
and an uneven recovery in downstream demand, overall industry              降，企業經營表現分化顯著。本集團憑藉
profitability contracted and the business performance of market            前瞻性的漿紙一體化、國際化及智能化戰
participants differed significantly. Benefiting from its forward-looking   略佈局，在複雜環境中展現強大韌性，成
pulp and paper integration, world-wide presence, and intelligent           功把握市場機遇，實現了盈利能力的穩步
transformation strategy, the Group has demonstrated strong                 提升與高質量增長。
resilience in a complex environment and managed to seize market
opportunities, making steady improvement in profitability and high-
quality growth.


For the year ended 31 December 2025, the Group recorded total              截至2025年12月31日止年度，本集團總
revenue of HK$26.6 billion, representing an increase of 2.5% from          收 入 比 去 年 上 升 2.5% 至 266 億 港 元 ， 年
the previous year, while the profit for the year increased by 43% to       度 盈 利 較 去 年 上 升 43% 至 19 億 港 元 。 年
HK$1.9 billion as compared to the previous year. Earnings per share        內每股盈利45.20港仙（2024年：30.56港
for the year were HK45.20 cents (2024: HK30.56 cents).                     仙）。


The board of directors has proposed a final dividend of HK9.3 cents        董事會建議2025年派發末期股息每股9.3
per share for 2025 (2024: HK4.5 cents).                                    港仙（2024年：4.5港仙）。


INDUSTRY AND BUSINESS REVIEW                                               行業及業務回顧

In 2025, the paper manufacturing industry in the PRC faced                 2025 年 ， 中 國 造 紙 行 業 不 僅 承 受 了 國 際
significant headwinds from the tariff barriers in international trade      貿易關稅壁壘的衝擊，亦處於產能過剩、
and has undergone the stage of “anti-involution” transformation            低價無序競爭導致利潤下降的「反內卷」轉
characterized by declining profits due to overcapacity and disorderly      型期。部分中小企業儘管產量同比增長，
low-price competition. Despite the increase in production output for       但營收與利潤總額錄得下跌。行業呈現
some small and medium-sized enterprises, they recorded decrease in         「紙漿依賴進口」、「龍頭「林漿紙一體化」
year-on-year revenue and profits. The industry exhibited several           佈局」及「中小企業面臨淘汰」的特徵。
trends of “reliance on imported pulp”, “implementation of forestry,
pulp, and paper integration by leading companies,” and “elimination
of small and medium-sized enterprises”.

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT (CONTINUED)
主 席 報 告（續）




During the year, pulp price generally exhibited a pattern of starting       年內，紙漿價格呈現前低後高、波動調整
low and ending high, with fluctuating adjustments throughout. In            的態勢。上半年，全球經濟增長預期轉
the first half of the year, lowered expectations for global economic        弱及新增產能釋放，導致紙漿價格承壓下
growth and the release of new production capacity posed downward            跌。年中至下半年，國內「反內卷」政策，
pressure on pulp prices. From mid-year to the second half of the            加上部分海外漿廠減產、轉產的消息使供
year, as a result of tightened pulp supply owing to the                     應趨緊，紙企通過漲價緩解成本壓力。而
implementation of domestic “anti-involution” policies, coupled with         國內「以舊換新」等促消費政策深入實施，
the news of downsizing production or switching to other production          有效拉動了包裝與消費用紙需求，亦支持
at some overseas pulp mills, paper manufacturing companies                  了整體紙品價格上升。
increased their prices to alleviate cost pressures. Meanwhile, the in-
depth implementation of stimulating policies on domestic
consumption such as the “trade-in” program has effectively boosted
the demand for packaging and consumer paper, supporting the
overall increase in paper prices.


In view of the complex and ever-changing industry environment, the          面對複雜多變的行業環境，本集團採取主
Group adopted proactive strategies to address challenges and seize          動策略以應對挑戰並捕捉機遇。本集團充
opportunities. Fully leveraging the advantage of its vertical integration   分發揮漿紙一體化的垂直整合優勢，透過
of pulp and paper operations, the Group effectively mitigated the           靈活調配內部紙漿產能與外部採購，有效
cost pressures caused by significant price fluctuations in raw materials    緩解了原材料價格大幅波動帶來的成本壓
through flexible adjustment of its capacity between internal pulp           力。同時，集團持續優化國內生產基地的
production and external procurement. Simultaneously, the Group              產能佈局與能源結構，進一步降低綜合生
continued to optimize the production layout and energy structure of         產成本。年內，本集團積極推進多項生產
its domestic production bases, further reducing its overall production      線技術改造與效率調試項目，提升了整體
costs. During the year, a number of technology upgrade and                  效率，為產能的全面釋放與效率提升奠定
efficiency enhancement projects were actively implemented for the           了堅實基礎。
Group’s production lines, which have increased its overall efficiency
and laid a solid foundation for the full release of production capacity
and efficiency enhancement.


In the face of uncertainties in geopolitics and trade policies, the         面對地緣政治與貿易政策的不確定性，本
Group’s overseas production bases in Vietnam and Malaysia have              集團設於越南及馬來西亞的海外生產基地
played a vital role. These bases not only serve the local and               發揮了重要作用。這些基地不僅服務於當
surrounding markets, but also act as bridgeheads for the Group to           地及周邊市場，更成為本集團拓展包括東
expand into emerging export markets along the “Belt and Road”,              南亞地區在內的「一帶一路」沿線及歐洲等
including Southeast Asia and Europe, achieving market diversification       新興出口市場的橋頭堡，實現了市場的多
and risk mitigation.                                                        元化與風險的分散化。

BUSINESS PROSPECTS                                                         業務前景

Packaging paper remains the Group’s core business. The demand for          包裝紙業務為本集團的主要核心業務。國
green packaging solutions was buoyant as the PRC government                家對塑料污染的治理力度只增不減，綠色
strengthens its commitment to addressing plastic pollution. The            包裝解決方案的需求旺盛。電商物流的持
continuous expansion of e-commerce logistics and the “trade-in”            續發展與消費品「以舊換新」政策，將直接
policy for consumer goods will directly drive the growth of demand         拉 動 高 品 質 包 裝 用 紙 的 增 長 。 2026 年 ，
for high-quality packaging paper. In 2026, the PRC government              中國將「提振消費」視為經濟工作首位，預
prioritized “boosting consumption” as its top economic goal, which is      期利好包裝紙業務。本集團憑藉一體化成
expected to favor the packaging paper business. With cost                  本優勢及智能化製造能力，將進一步搶佔
advantages through integration and intelligent manufacturing               市場份額，擴大盈利空間。
capabilities, the Group’s packaging paper business is well positioned
to capture further market share and increase its profit margins.


Looking ahead, our key strategic focus will be on the expansion of         出口市場拓展將是未來的戰略重點。我們
export markets. We will continue to expand the regional coverage of        將繼續深化東南亞生產基地的區域輻射作
our production bases in Southeast Asia and actively explore                用，並深入探索在「一帶一路」新興市場的
investment opportunities in emerging markets along the “Belt and           投資機會。通過數字化與智能化手段提升
Road”. By upgrading our industrial chain with digital and intelligent      產業鏈水平，為全球客戶提供更具競爭力
technologies, we aim at providing more competitive supply chain            的產業鏈服務，將國際化佈局真正轉化為
services to our global customers, truly transforming our world-wide        持續的增長動力。
presence into a sustainable growth engine.

The Group has established a sound risk management and internal           本集團設有完善的風險管理及內部控制系

of internal processes. Independent third party consultant is engaged     三方機構為本集團進行全面的風險評估及

The Board                 Formulate the overall development direction and governance policies, and set
                          specific and measurable visions for ESG (including climate-related) strategies. The
                          Board reviews ESG-related matters semi-annually, including: assessing the
                          achievement of existing targets and determining future targets; regularly
                          reviewing and monitoring the implementation and effectiveness of governance
                          strategies, and making adjustments based on the Group’s performance and the
                          external environment; and reviewing ESG-related policies and reports. Also, the
                          Board regularly assesses whether its members possess the skills and competence
                          required to supervise climate-related matters and improve professional skills
                          through ongoing training. In supervising the strategies, major transactions and
                          risk management processes of the Group, the Board considers ESG (including
                          climate-related) risks and opportunities, ensuring the effective operation of
                          internal controls and risk management to mitigate risks, seize opportunities and
                          promote sustainable development.
董事會                       制訂整體發展方向與管治方針，並就ESG（包括氣候相關）策略設定具體且
                          可衡量的願景。董事會每半年審議ESG相關事項，包括：評估既定目標的
                          執行情況並確認未來目標；定期審視與監督管治策略的執行與成效，並按
                          本集團表現與外部環境作出調整；以及審閱ESG相關政策與報告。同時，
                          董事會定期評估成員是否具備監督氣候相關事務所需之技能與勝任能力，
                          並透過持續培訓提升專業水平。在監督本集團策略、重大交易與風險管理
                          程序時，董事會考量ESG（包括氣候相關）風險與機遇，確保內部監控與風
                          險管理有效運作，以降低風險、把握機遇並推動可持續發展。

ESG office                      To manage the climate-related affairs of the Group on a systematic basis, an ESG
                                office has been established to facilitate specific measures and cultivate ESG
                                culture in all departments. Such office is responsible for the formulation and
                                implementation of ESG management regulations for each department,
                                clarification of responsibilities and measures, supervision and provision of
                                guidance on the implementation of ESG (including climate-related) policies in
                                daily operations, promotion of ESG culture and activities, setting of measurement
                                standards for ESG (including climate-related) performance, and regular report of
                                key data and progress to the ESG dedicated working team. Moreover, the ESG
                                contact person coordinates with internal functional departments for the
                                integration of climate-related monitoring measures and processes to ensure
                                alignment with overall operational targets.
ESG辦公室                          為系統化管理本集團的氣候相關事務，本集團設立ESG辦公室，於各部門
                                推動具體措施並培養ESG文化，負責制定並落實各部門之ESG管理規定、明
                                確責任與措施、監督與指導日常運作中貫徹ESG（包括氣候相關）政策、推
                                動ESG文化與活動、為ESG（包括氣候相關）績效設立衡量標準並定期向ESG
                                工作小組匯報關鍵數據與進度，且由ESG聯絡人協同內部職能部門整合氣
                                候相關監控措施與流程，以確保與整體營運目標一致。

production model of converting waste paper into environmentally-        的綠色生產模式不受影響。
friendly products is not affected.


Being well aware that environmental protection is an important basis    本集團深明環境保護是可持續發展的重要
for its sustainable development, the Group has adopted a number of      基礎，因此採取多項改進措施及監控方法
improvement and monitoring measures to ensure performance of            確保履行對保護環境的責任。本集團採取
our environmental responsibilities. The Group has established various   的各項環保政策與措施包括：
environmental protection policies and measures including:

Setting Up Environmental Team
成立環保小組

Led by our Chief Executive Officer, the environmental team comprises members having the relevant expertise,
including senior flue gas treatment experts, sewage treatment technicians, environmental monitoring professionals
and environmental protection technicians so as to ensure effective implementation of the following measures.
由首席執行官親自帶領，由合資格專才組成，包括高級煙氣治理師、污水處理人員、環境監測人員、環境保護專業
技術人員等，保證下列相關環保措施得以有效執行。

Duties of Environmental Team
環保小組職責

•    Holding internal control meetings regularly to review environmental issues in the plants, to update themselves
     with applicable environmental laws and regulations and to give advice on any improvements;
•    On an ongoing basis, the team engages themselves in environmental protection measures to be taken in
     production processes, including those employed to lower water consumption, treat sewage water, save energy,
     apply more environment-friendly exhaust gas treatment technologies and emission monitoring to ensure
     continued compliance with high environmental protection standards.
•    定期召開內部監控會議，旨在審閱有關廠房內的環保事宜，通知各成員環保法律及規定的更新條文，並為存
     在改善空間的環節提供適當建議；
•    不斷完善生產過程中的環保措施，包括減少耗水量、應用更有效的廢水處理技術、節省能源耗用量、採用更
     環保的廢氣處理技術及排放監控等，確保可持續地達到高環保標準。

Environmental Policies and Measures
環保政策與措施

•    set up policies for emissions treatment to provide clear targets and state the responsibilities and division of
     labour for environmental protection measures adopted by the Group;
•    formulate guidelines for energy-saving and reduce emission at the source;
•    as part of the various efforts to make us one of the companies which best complies with environmental
     protection principles in the world, our different plants have employed technologies with high efficiency to
     reduce emission and keep our emission below the latest emission standards laid down by the government as
     well as internationally applicable environmental management standards.
•    設立排放物處理辦法，為本集團採納之環保措施提供明確的目標和職責分工；
•    訂立節約能源指引，從源頭減排；
•    各廠房利用高效率技術降低排放，保持低於政府最新排放標準的排放水準，對標國際環境管理標準，並努力
     成為全球最符合環保原則的公司之一。

Wastes                                                                      廢棄物
The wastes generated in our operations were mainly non-hazardous            本集團營運所產生的廢物主要為造紙過程
ones produced in the course of paper-making, together with a small          中所產生的無害廢棄物，亦有少量日常行
quantity of wastes due to routine office work. In order to reduce and       政辦公所產生的廢棄物。針對造紙過程中
dispose of waste produced in the paper-making process, the Group            所產生的廢棄物的減少和處理，本集團已
has established the “Guidelines for Waste Management” to classify           設立《廢棄物管理指導書》對本集團所有活
wastes of all activities and clarify the workflow of processing. Policies   動產生的廢棄物進行分類，明確其處理操
also provide for guidelines for the storage, transport and disposal of      作流程。政策亦為廢棄物的存放、運輸及
wastes and require them to be recorded to ensure that treatment             處置提供指引並要求記錄在案，確保處理
process is in compliance with rules and is safe.                            過程安全合規。


The Group has principally disposed of wastes generated from its             本集團主要通過以下三種方式處理廢棄
paper-making operations in three ways:                                      物：


•     Sewage treatment facilities in plants will filter sewage              •    工廠的污水處理設施過濾造紙過程
      generated from the paper-making process. This process would                中所產生的污水，平均每月會產生
      generate an average of over 10,000 tonnes of sludge. It is then            過萬噸污泥。污泥經處理及深度脫
      burnt in boilers after treatment and deep drying. The energy               水後被送至鍋爐焚燒，通過迴圈流
      generated by burning the waste in a circulating fluidised bed              化床鍋爐焚燒的廢物所產生的能量
      boilers could be reused in the paper-making process and                    用於造紙生產，不但不會造成二次
      therefore, not only secondary pollution would be avoided but               污染，還節省了能源消耗；
      also saving energy;


•     The Group used lots of waste paper as a raw material in its           •    本集團在造紙生產過程中使用大量
      paper-making operations, paper-making residue was left                     廢紙作為原料，廢紙經提取有用的
      behind after extraction of useful fibres for the paper-making              造紙纖維後剩下造紙廢渣。本集團
      process. As the residue contained different impurities, the                把造紙廢渣引進洗水線提取有價值
      residue was carried to a washing line where reusable metal                 雜質，如鐵絲及塑膠等，並將此等
      wires and plastics in the residue were collected and sold to               物料出售予回收商進行回收再造；
      recyclers;


•     Established “Directory of Hazardous Wastes”, so as to                 •    編 製《 危 險 廢 棄 物 匯 總 表 》， 明 確 分
      segregate hazardous wastes before processing or disposal of                類危險廢棄物，繼而處理或出售可
      the recyclable.                                                            回收部分。

wastes, striving to meet the standard of processing solid wastes,          100% 達 到《 廣 東 省 嚴 控 廢 物 處 理 行 政 許
liquid wastes and hazardous wastes stipulated in the “Measures of          可實施辦法》和《危險廢物轉移聯單管理辦

Climate-related risks and opportunity management                                             氣候相關風險及機遇管理
The Group recognizes the potential impact of climate risks and                               本集團已意識到氣候風險及機遇對於所在
opportunities on business in the industry and is committed to                                行業的業務的潛在影響，並致力於將氣候
integrating the management of climate risks and opportunities into                           風險及機遇的管理融入到我們的治理框架
its governance framework, with a view to ensuring the long-term                              中，以確保業務的長期氣候韌性和集團的

the Group. During the year, the Group has made climate-related                               交所最新的《環境、社會及管治報告守則》

and Governance Reporting Code of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange,                               集團在氣候風險和機遇的管理的透明度。
further increasing the transparency of the Group’s management of
climate risks and opportunities.

      conduct any quantitative scenario analysis for external disclosure during the               部披露的量化情景分析。後續將完善資料盤點
      reporting period. Going forward, we will improve our data collection and                    與方法並逐步提升披露程度。
      methodology and gradually enhance our disclosure.

CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
The directors of the Company are responsible for the preparation of       貴公司董事須負責根據香港會計師公會頒
the consolidated financial statements that give a true and fair view in   布的香港財務報告準則會計準則及香港公
accordance with HKFRS Accounting Standards as issued by the               司條例披露規定編製真實而公平的綜合財
HKICPA and the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong                   務報表，以及董事釐為必須的內部監控，
Companies Ordinance, and for such internal control as the directors       以使編製的綜合財務報表不存在由於欺詐
determine is necessary to enable the preparation of consolidated          或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述。
financial statements that are free from material misstatement,
whether due to fraud or error.


In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are     在編製綜合財務報表時，董事負責評
responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going      估   貴集團持續經營的能力，並在適用情
concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern      況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以及使
and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors      用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事有意
either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have      將   貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他
no realistic alternative but to do so.                                    實際的替代方案。

      HKFRS 18 Presentation and Disclosure in Financial Statements ,           香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 18 號財 務 報 表
      which sets out requirements on presentation and disclosures in           中的呈列及披露 載列財務報表的呈
      financial statements, will replace HKAS 1 Presentation of                報及披露規定，將取代香港會計準

      while carrying forward many of the requirements in HKAS 1,               財務報告準則會計準則於延續香港
      introduces new requirements to present specified categories              會計準則第1號多項規定的同時引
      and defined subtotals in the statement of profit or loss; provide        入新規定，要求於損益表呈列指定
      disclosures on management-defined performance measures in                類別及經界定小計，於財務報表附
      the notes to the financial statements and improve aggregation            註中提供管理層界定績效衡量的披
      and disaggregation of information to be disclosed in the                 露，並改善財務報表中呈列的匯總
      financial statements. In addition, some HKAS 1 paragraphs                及分類資料。此外，香港會計準則
      have been moved to HKAS 8 Accounting Policies, Changes in                第1號若干段落已移至香港會計準則
      Accounting Estimates and Errors and HKFRS 7 Financial                    第8號會計政策、會計估計變動及錯
      Instruments: Disclosures . Minor amendments to HKAS 7                    誤 及香港財務報告準則第7號金融工
      Statement of Cash Flows and HKAS 33 Earnings per Share are               具：披露 。香港會計準則第7號現金

                                                                               盈利 亦有輕微修訂。

         financial statements

          The consolidated financial statements have been                    綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計
          prepared in accordance with HKFRSs issued by the                   師公會頒布之香港財務報告準
          HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the                      則編製。就編製綜合財務報表
          consolidated financial statements, information is                  而言，倘資料可以合理預期會
          considered material if such information is reasonably              影響主要使用者作出決策，則
          expected to influence decisions made by primary users. In          此等資料被認為重大。此外，
          addition, the consolidated financial statements include            綜合財務報表包括聯交所證券
          applicable disclosures required by the Rules Governing             上 市 規 則（「 上 市 規 則 」）及 香
          the Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the               港公司條例規定之適用披露資
          “Listing Rules”) and the Hong Kong Companies                       料。
          Ordinance.

      3.2 Material accounting policies information                             3.2 重大會計政策資料

          Basis of consolidation                                                 綜合賬目基準
          The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                  本綜合財務報表包含本公司以
          financial statements of the Company and entities                       及由本公司及其附屬公司控制
          controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is             的實體之財務報表。取得控制
          achieved where the Company:                                            權是指本公司：


          •     has power over the investee;                                     •   對被投資方行使權力；


          •     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its          •   因參與被投資方之業務而
                involvement with the investee; and                                   獲得或有權獲得可變回
                                                                                     報；及


          •     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.          •   有能力行使其權力而影響
                                                                                     其回報。


          The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                     倘事實及情況顯示上述三項控
          investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are            制元素其中一項或多項改變，
          changes to one or more of the three elements of control                則本集團會重新評估其是否仍
          listed above.                                                          然控制被投資方。


          When the Group has less than a majority of the voting                  倘本集團於被投資公司並無大
          rights of an investee, it has power over the investee when             多數投票權，則本集團僅在投
          the voting rights are sufficient to give it the practical              票權足以賦予其實際能力可
          ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee              單方面指示被投資公司的相關
          unilaterally. The Group considers all relevant facts and               活動時方對被投資公司擁有權
          circumstances in assessing whether or not the Group’s                  力。在評估本集團於被投資公
          voting rights in an investee are sufficient to give it power,          司的投票權是否足以賦予其權
          including:                                                             力時，本集團考慮一切相關事
                                                                                 實及情況，包括：


          •     the size of the Group’s holding of voting rights                 •   本集團所持投票權的規模
                relative to the size and dispersion of holdings of the               相對於其他投票權持有人
                other vote holders;                                                  所持投票權的規模及分散
                                                                                     程度；

          A performance obligation represents a good (or a bundle                履 約 責 任 指 不 同 的 貨 品（ 或 一
          of goods) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods that          籃 子 貨 品 ）或 大 致 上 相 同 之 一
          are substantially the same.                                            系列不同的貨品。


          A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to              合約負債指本集團已從客戶收
          transfer goods to a customer for which the Group has                   取代價（或代價金額已到期）而
          received consideration (or an amount of consideration is               本集團有責任去轉移貨品予客
          due) from the customer.                                                戶。


          Revenue from sale of packaging paper, tissue paper and                 來自銷售包裝紙、衛生紙及木
          pulp is recognised at a point in time when the customer                漿之收入於客戶取得貨品的控
          obtains control of goods.                                              制權時按一個時間點確認。


          Leases                                                                 租賃
          The Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a                 本集團根據香港財務報告準則
          lease based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at                        第16號之定義於合約初始時評
          inception of the contract. Such contract will not be                   估一份合約是否（或包含）一項
          reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the                      租賃。除非合約之條款及條件
          contract are subsequently changed.                                     其後改變，此合約將不會再評
                                                                                 估。


          The Group as a lessee                                                  本集團作為承租人
          Short-term leases                                                      短期租賃
          The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                     本集團對租約期自開始日期計
          exemption that have a lease term of 12 months or less                  12個月或更短者且並無包含購
          from the commencement date and do not contain a                        買權之租賃應用短期租賃確認
          purchase option. Lease payments on short-term leases                   豁免。短期租賃之租賃付款按
          are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis unless              直線法確認為開支，除非另有
          another systematic is more representative of the time                  其他系統化基準更能代表耗用
          pattern in which economic benefits from the leased                     租賃資產的經濟利益的時間模
          assets are consumed.                                                   式則作別論。

          Lease liabilities                                                     租賃負債
          At the commencement date of a lease, the Group                        於租賃開始日期，本集團以該
          recognises and measures the lease liability at the present            日未付租賃付款現值確認及計
          value of lease payments that are unpaid at that date. In              量租賃負債。於計算租賃付
          calculating the present value of lease payments, the                  款現值時，因隱含在租賃中之
          Group uses the incremental borrowing rate at the lease                利率未能明顯確定，本集團會
          commencement date as the interest rate implicit in the                採用租賃開始日期之漸進借貸
          lease is not readily determinable. The incremental                    率。增量借款利率取決於租賃
          borrowing rate depends on the term, currency and start                的期限、貨幣及開始日期，並
          date of the lease and is determined based on a series of              根據一系列輸入因素確定，包
          inputs including: risk-free rate based on government                  括基於政府債券利率的無風險
          bond rates; a country-specific risk adjustment; a credit              利率； 特定國家的風險調整；
          risk adjustment based on bond yields; and an entity-                  基於債券收益率的信用風險調
          specific adjustment whether the risk profile of the entity            整； 以及特定實體的調整，即
          that enters into the lease is different to that of the Group          訂立租賃協議的實體的風險狀
          and whether the lease benefit from a guarantee from the               況是否與本集團的風險狀況不
          Group.                                                                同，以及該租賃協議是否受益
                                                                                於本集團的擔保。


          The lease payments include fixed payments (including in-              租 賃 付 款 包 括 固 定 付 款（ 包 括
          substance fixed payments) less any lease incentives                   實 質 上 固 定 付 款 ）減 去 任 何 應
          receivable.                                                           收租賃補助。


          After the commencement date, lease liabilities are                    於開始日期後，租賃負債按利
          adjusted by interest accretion and lease payments.                    息增長及租賃付款作調整。

          Foreign currencies (Continued)                                         （續）
                                                                                外幣
          For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial             就呈列綜合財務報表而言，本
          statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s                 集團經營業務之資產及負債乃
          operations are translated into the presentation currency              採用各報告期末當日之匯率換
          of the Group (i.e. HK$) using exchange rates prevailing at            算 為 本 集 團 之 列 賬 貨 幣（ 即 港
          the end of each reporting period. Income and expenses                 元 ）， 而 收 入 及 支 出 項 目 乃 按
          items are translated at the average exchange rates for the            該期間之平均匯率進行換算，
          period, unless exchange rates fluctuate significantly                 除非匯率於該期間內出現大幅
          during the period, in which case, the exchange rates at               波動則作別論，於此情況下，
          the date of transactions are used. Exchange differences               則採用於交易當日之匯率。所
          arising, if any, are recognised in other comprehensive                產生之匯兌差額（如有）乃於其
          income and accumulated in equity under the heading of                 他全面收益確認及於匯兌儲備
          translation reserve (attributed to non-controlling interests          項 下 累 計 權 益（ 適 用 時 歸 入 非
          as appropriate).                                                      控股權益）。


          Borrowing costs                                                       借貸成本
          Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,             與購置、建造或生產合資格資
          construction or production of qualifying assets, which are            產（ 需 一 段 長 時 間 方 可 作 擬 定
          assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to          用 途 或 可 供 銷 售 的 資 產 ）直 接
          get ready for their intended use or sale, are added to the            有關之借貸成本均加至該等資
          cost of those assets until such time as the assets are                產之成本，直至該等資產大致
          substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                   可作其擬定用途或可供銷售為
                                                                                止。


          Any specific borrowing that remain outstanding after the              於相關資產可用於擬定用途或
          related asset is ready for its intended use or sale is                出售後仍未償還的任何特定借

                                                                                 率計算。

          deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                 認。倘暫時差額乃由初始確認

          The Group performs impairment assessment under                          本集團於金融資產就預期信貸
          expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                  虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進
          which are subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS                  行減值評估乃視乎香港財務準
          9 (including trade and other receivables, loan to a joint               則第9號項下的減值評估（包括
          venture, amounts due from related companies and bank                    應收貿易及其他賬款、合營企
          balances). The amount of ECL is updated at each                         業貸款、應收關連公司款項及
          reporting date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial          銀 行 結 餘 ）。 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 金
          recognition.                                                            額於每個報告日期更新以反映
                                                                                  自初始確認以來之信貸風險變
                                                                                  動。


          Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all               終身預期信貸虧損指由所有可
          possible default events over the expected life of the                   能違約事件超出相關工具預期
          relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                    壽命將引致之預期信貸虧損。
          ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                    相 反 ， 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損
          expected to result from default events that are possible                （「12個月預期信貸虧損」）指終
          within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment is                身預期信貸虧損預期從可能於
          done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                        報告日期後12個月內的違約事
          experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the               件引致之部分。所作評估基於
          debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                  本集團過往信貸虧損記錄，並
          of past events and the current conditions at the reporting              調整債務人的特定因素，普遍
          date as well as the forecast of future economic                         經濟狀況及同時對於報告日期
          conditions.                                                             之過去事件及現況及預測未來
                                                                                  經濟狀況之評估。

          The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                      本集團通常就應收貿易賬款確
          receivables.                                                            認終身預期信貸虧損。

          For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                  就所有其他工具，本集團以相
          allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a                     等 於 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 計
          significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          量虧損撥備，除非自初始確認
          in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                    後信貸風險顯著增加，本集團
          assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                 則確認終身預期信貸虧損。評
          is based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk             估是否應確認終身預期信貸虧
          of a default occurring since initial recognition.                       損乃基於自初始確認後發生違
                                                                                  約的可能性或風險是否顯著增
                                                                                  加。

          (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                               (i)   信貸風險顯著增加

                In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                      於評估自初始確認後信貸
                significantly since initial recognition, the Group                      風險是否顯著增加時，本
                compares the risk of a default occurring on the                         集團將於報告日期金融工
                financial instrument as at the reporting date with                      具發生違約之風險與於初
                the risk of a default occurring on the financial                        始確認日期金融工具發生
                instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In                    違約之風險作比較。作出
                making this assessment, the Group considers both                        評估時，本集團同時考慮
                quantitative and qualitative information that is                        合理及有理據的定量及定
                reasonable and supportable, including historical                        性資料，包括過往經驗及
                experience and forward-looking information that is                      無需花費成本或精力而可
                available without undue cost or effort. Forward-                        得之前瞻性資料。所考慮
                looking information considered includes the future                      的前瞻性資料包括來自經
                prospects of the industries in which the Group’s                        濟專家的報告、金融分析
                debtors operate, obtained from economic expert                          師、政府機構及其他類似
                reports, financial analysts, governmental bodies and                    組織有關本集團債務人營
                other similar organisations, as well as consideration                   運行業的未來前景，以及
                of various external sources of actual and forecast                      與本集團核心業務相關的
                economic information that relate to the Group’s                         各種外部實際及預測經濟
                core operations.                                                        資料的考量因素。

                  has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, or in the                     應收貿易賬款，當金額乃

                  over two years past due, whichever occurs sooner.                      為準，本集團撇銷金融資
                  Financial assets written off may still be subject to                   產。金融資產的撇銷可能

                  appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                   慮法律建議。撇銷構成剔
                  event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in                     除確認事項。任何其後回
                  profit or loss.                                                        撥於損益確認。

          (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確
                                                                                      認


                The measurement of ECL is a function of the                           預期信貸虧損計量具違約
                probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                  可 能 性 ， 默 認 虧 損（ 即 違
                magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and the                  約 的 損 失 幅 度 ）及 違 約 曝
                exposure at default. The assessment of the                            光的功能。違約可能性及
                probability of default and loss given default is based                默認虧損的評估乃基於過
                on historical data and forward-looking information.                   往數據及前瞻性資料。預
                Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                            期信貸虧損的估計反映不
                probability-weighted amount that is determined                        偏倚及概率加權金額乃按
                with the respective risks of default occurring as the                 發生違約相關風險的比重
                weights.                                                              來釐定。


                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                      一般而言，預期信貸虧損
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                   為根據合約所有應付本集
                accordance with the contract and the cash flows                       團合約現金流量與本集團
                that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                      於初始確認時預期收到，
                the effective interest rate determined at initial                     按實際利率折現的現金流
                recognition.                                                          量的差額。

19. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                     19. 投資物業

      The Group leases out commercial and industrial properties                 本集團根據經營租賃按每月付租出
      under operating leases with rentals payable monthly. The leases           租商業及工業物業。租賃通常初始
      typically run for an initial period of 1 to 10 years, with unilateral     年期為1至10年，承租人單方面有權
      rights to extend the lease beyond initial period held by lessees          延續租賃至初始年期外。大部分租
      only. Majority of the lease contracts contain annual lease                賃合約在租期內固定年度租賃付款。
      payment that are fixed over the lease term.


      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of          本集團在租賃安排並無承受外幣風
      the lease arrangements, as all leases are denominated in the              險，因所有租賃乃按集團實體相關
      respective functional currencies of group entities. The lease             功能貨幣計值。租賃合約並無包含
      contracts do not contain residual value guarantee and lessee’s            剩餘價值保證及承租人於租期完結
      option to purchase the property at the end of lease term.                 時購入物業之權利。

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              35. 資本風險管理

     The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the          本集團管理其資本以確保本集團的
     Group will be able to continue as a going concern while               實體可持續經營業務，並透過最佳
     maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation        債務及權益比例為權益持有人提供
     of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy          最高回報。本集團的整體政策維持
     remains unchanged from prior year.                                    與去年一致。


     The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which           本集團的資本結構包括債務，當中
     include the bank borrowings and lease liabilities as disclosed in     包括分別於附註30及28披露之銀行
     notes 30 and 28 respectively, and equity attributable to equity       借貸及租賃負債，以及本公司權益
     holders of the Company, comprising issued share capital and           持有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及
     various reserves.                                                     多項儲備）。


     The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a        本公司董事不時檢討資本結構。作
     regular basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the     為檢討的一部分，董事考慮資本成
     cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of           本以及與各類資本有關的風險。根
     capital. Based on recommendations of the directors, the Group         據董事的建議，本集團將通過派付
